Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The tissue operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 668. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 400 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the tissue decomposition. By minimality, the element a^668 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 400-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the tissue structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 155. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
